- **multi-agent-workflow**: Bound concurrent Cargo builds to the machine's available CPU, memory, and disk. Use the build limiter your setup provides, if any, and never hold one build lock while waiting for another. Set `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS` explicitly when several worktrees are active. Prefer `cargo check -p &lt;crate&gt;` and avoid release builds unless the task requires performance measurements. Local builds do not use sccache. ([cinnabar-mc/cinnabar](https://github.com/cinnabar-mc/cinnabar/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/multi-agent-workflow.md) · ★714)
